Every word of law in this repository comes from these sources. Lexifina reads them and records each act's dated versions, and the laws that made each change, in one corpus file. This repository is built from that file.

Other counts:

## Dates

Each commit is dated at noon (Europe/Berlin) on the day the change took effect. The other dates, enacted and published, are trailers.

When a law gives no date of its own, the default rule applies: Verfassung für das Land Nordrhein-Westfalen Art. 71 Abs. 3: "Jedes Gesetz und jede Rechtsverordnung soll den Tag des Inkrafttretens bestimmen." Each version here is dated by the day RECHT.NRW gives as the start of its validity ("Gültig ab"). https://recht.nrw.de/lrgv/gesetz/16012026-verfassung-fuer-das-land-nordrhein-westfalen

## How the history is built

`tools/build.py` turns the corpus into this history in one pass: one commit per law, on the day it took effect. The same corpus always gives the same commit hashes. `tools/check_repo.py` reads every version back from git and compares it with the corpus. `tools/on.py` shows the law as it stood on any date. The corpus format is described in `tools/SCHEMA.md`.

- Corpus: sha256 `7027e537b78f8fc014a96603c0a869524872aa5189fccbeacc7d92546d0652cd` (35944439 bytes).
- Builder blob hashes (`git hash-object`): `build.py` `a4faa25af85facb64b956b372f6b528aafdd7ec1`, `check.py` `3d00541d258745461ab52b1b0cb4e1716a27a95d`, `on.py` `644a6c0857fbcc72562032b58d57aa113fee0641`.
- Python 3.12.10; git version 2.46.0.windows.1.

## Checks

1164 versions of 877 acts read back from git on the day each took effect and on the day before: 0 mismatches. Trailers: 0 commits whose trailers git cannot parse. Health: 0 merge commits on main, 0 commit dates that go backwards, 0 commits dated after the build.

No second build of this corpus has been compared yet.

## Decisions made by hand

- Every Fassung RECHT.NRW shows as structured text is one version here, dated by its "Gültig ab" day. Older Fassungen that the portal shows only as legacy files (PDF or old HTML) are not used, so each act starts with the oldest structured Fassung, credited to the publisher as the oldest version available.
- The provisions that changed between two versions are credited to the amending act(s) the newer version's Änderungshistorie lists as coming into force on that version's first day; if it lists none for that day, the change is labelled unexplained.
- One file per Artikel or Paragraph; headings of Teile and Abschnitte stand where the portal prints them (at the end of the provision before). Footnotes are left out; tables are one line per row, cells separated by " | "; numbered lists whose numbers the browser supplies are written with those numbers.

- Every Fassung RECHT.NRW shows as structured text is one version here, dated by its "Gültig ab" day. Older Fassungen that the portal shows only as legacy files (PDF or old HTML) are not used, so each act starts with the oldest structured Fassung, credited to the publisher as the oldest version available.
- The provisions that changed between two versions are credited to the amending act(s) the newer version's Änderungshistorie lists as coming into force on that version's first day; if it lists none for that day, the change is labelled unexplained.
- One file per Artikel or Paragraph; headings of Teile and Abschnitte stand where the portal prints them (at the end of the provision before). Footnotes are left out; tables are one line per row, cells separated by " | "; numbered lists whose numbers the browser supplies are written with those numbers.

## What AI did and did not do

The scripts that read the sources and build this repository were written by Claude Code running Claude Opus 5.5 (`claude-opus-5-5`) under Lexifina's direction.

No model wrote, translated or corrected legal text. No model runs at build time. Every word of law in this repository comes from the official sources above.

The commit messages are written by `build.py` from the source data.

Model use at data-build time:
- Claude Code (claude-opus-5-5) wrote Lexifina's code and chose the sources. No model output is used as legal text, dates or credits: every word comes from the RECHT.NRW pages, and every date and credit from the portal's own validity dates and Änderungshistorie.
